echo ----; gh issue view 1 --json body --jq .body"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_01UxMx2z9uEvwjyphGFcWfJX":{"cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"3010cc2f-44e9-4f74-a2e9-8a6c631dffd7","apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfyX4V9LLrfr3DXYsrVdK","requestedModel":"claude-sonnet-5-5","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:implement","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"8c594b60-bb2f-4049-bdf7-e32d2d0292dc","timestamp":"2026-10-13T03:22:55.227Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"68fe07ff-a133-4d44-8ff0-716e95c03e55","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"b36ba01a-82f5-45f0-a1a0-30321be8bf78","version":"2.1.296","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"8c594b60-bb2f-4049-bdf7-e32d2d0292dc","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"bf41cbd0-9b6f-4cb6-b4f0-9851f6e5a70f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UxMx2z9uEvwjyphGFcWfJX","type":"tool_result","content":"T05: Admin dashboard and filterable content list\n## Parent\n\nSpec: #1\n\n## What to build\n\nThe Manager sees a dashboard with content counts and a content list filterable by\ntype and status; the Editor sees the same dashboard scoped to what they can act on.\nEvery content editor (Pages and Posts) includes a live Markdown preview before\nsaving.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Dashboard shows counts of News (draft vs. published) and each permanent page's\n      status.\n- [ ] Content list is filterable by type (News/Pages) and by status (draft/published).\n- [ ] Editor's content list is scoped to News only — no permanent-page rows, matching\n      their permission boundary.\n- [ ] Every content editor (Pages and Posts) includes a live Markdown preview before\n      saving.\n- [ ] `tests/test_admin_console.py` (new) covers dashboard counts, list filtering,\n      and editor-scoped list content.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #3 (T02: Permanent pages) — dashboard/list need Page data to summarize.\n- #5 (T04: News posts) — dashboard/list need Post data to summarize.\n\n----\n## Problem Statement\n\nLa Once Mil (the Michelin-starred flagship taquería at Monte Everest 780, Lomas de\nChapultepec, Mexico City) has no usable web presence. Its official domain\n(`laoncemil.com`) is a permanent \"under construction\" page, and its real online\npresence — Instagram, TikTok, a Linktree — buries the one thing most visitors need\n(address, hours, \"is it walk-in only\") under a scrolling feed. A first-time visitor\ndrawn by Michelin coverage, or a repeat local visitor checking today's hours, has no\nfast way to get that information without digging through a social feed they may not\nalready follow. There is also no simple way for the restaurant to post a dated\nannouncement (e.g. news, seasonal changes) without it disappearing into that same feed.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with a local admin console (never exposed to the internet, per ADR-001)\nand a static public site exported to GitHub Pages. The public Home page leads with\nverified address, hours, a directions link, and a prominent Call button, because that\nis the one glance a visitor needs. A Manager role can publish News, edit the\npermanent pages (Home, Menu, Our Story), and manage user accounts; an Editor role can\ndraft News for the Manager to review and publish, but cannot publish anything, edit\nthe permanent pages, or touch user accounts. Publishing marks content as published in\nthe local CMS only — the public GitHub Pages site reflects that change only after\n`cms publish` exports to `site/` and that export is deployed.\n\n## User Stories\n\n1. As the Manager, I want to log in with a hashed password, so that only I can reach\n   the admin console.\n2. As the Manager, I want to log out, so that a shared or public machine doesn't stay\n   signed in.\n3. As anyone entering a wrong password, I want a clear rejection message with no\n   account created or session started, so that login failures are unambiguous.\n4. As the Editor, I want to log in with my own hashed-password account, so that I can\n   draft News without sharing the Manager's credentials.\n5. As an anonymous visitor, I want to be redirected to login when I try to reach any\n   admin route, so that the admin console is never reachable without authentication.\n6. As the Manager, I want the Editor's role to visibly differ from mine (different\n   nav options, different available actions), so that the two roles are genuinely\n   distinct, not just a label.\n7. As the Editor, I want to be blocked (403 or redirect, never a silent no-op) if I\n   submit a direct HTTP request to a Manager-only route (publish, permanent-page edit,\n   user management) even without using the UI, so that the restriction can't be\n   bypassed by hand-crafting a request.\n8. As the Manager, I want to create, edit, and delete News posts (title, slug,\n   Markdown body, draft/published status, author, timestamps), so that I can keep the\n   public site current.\n9. As the Editor, I want to create and edit News posts as drafts, so that I can\n   prepare an announcement for the Manager's review.\n10. As the Editor, I want my save actions to always leave the post in draft status\n    (never published), so that I cannot accidentally or deliberately publish content.\n11. As the Manager, I want to publish or unpublish a News post, so that I control what\n    reaches the public site.\n12. As the Manager, I want the News list to be filterable by status (draft/published),\n    so that I can find what still needs review.\n13. As the Manager, I want to edit the Home, Menu, and Our Story pages (each with\n    title, slug, Markdown body, draft/published status, author, timestamps, matching\n    the Posts capability's fields), so that I control the site's permanent content.\n14. As the Editor, I want the permanent-page editors to be unreachable to me (no UI\n    entry point, and a 403/redirect if I try the route directly), so that the\n    \"Editor never touches permanent pages\" rule is enforced, not just hidden.\n15. As a visitor, I want a published permanent page to appear in the public site's\n    navigation once it's exported, so that I can reach Menu and Our Story from Home.\n16. As the Manager, I want a dashboard that shows counts of News posts (draft vs.\n    published) and permanent-page status, so that I can see the site's state at a\n    glance.\n17. As the Manager, I want a content list I can filter by type (News/Pages) and by\n    status, so that I can navigate a growing set of content without scrolling\n    everything.\n18. As the Manager or Editor, I want a Markdown preview in the editor before saving,\n    so that I can see how the rendered content will look.\n19. As the Manager, I want to create a new user account (email, password, role), so\n    that I can add an Editor when needed.\n20. As the Manager, I want to change a user's role, so that I can promote or demote\n    without deleting and recreating the account.\n21. As the Manager, I want to deactivate a user, so that someone who shouldn't have\n    access anymore loses it without deleting their historical authorship record.\n22. As a deactivated user, I want my login attempt to be refused with a clear message,\n    even with the correct password, so that deactivation is actually enforced.\n23. As the Editor, I want the user-management routes to be unreachable to me (no UI\n    entry point, and a 403/redirect on direct request), so that only the Manager\n    controls accounts.\n24. As a visitor, I want the public Home page to show verified address, hours, a\n    directions link, and a prominent Call button, prioritized above other content\n    (signature dish, walk-in-only note), so that I get the \"where and when\" answer\n    immediately.\n25. As a visitor, I want the public site to show only published content — no draft\n    ever appears — so that I never see unfinished or unreviewed material.\n26. As a visitor, I want any Markdown-authored content (News, Home, Menu, Our Story)\n    to render safely, with scripts and dangerous attributes stripped, so that the\n    public site can't be used to inject malicious content even if a draft author\n    pasted something unsafe.\n27. As a visitor, I want every link and asset on the exported site to use a relative\n    path (never root-absolute), so that the site works correctly when served from a\n    GitHub Pages project subfolder.\n28. As a visitor, I want every published page — including Home — reachable by\n    clicking through the site's navigation, so that no published content is an\n    unlinked orphan.\n29. As the Manager, I want `cms publish` to regenerate `site/` from only the current\n    published content, so that unpublishing something and re-running publish actually\n    removes it from the export.\n30. As the Manager, I want a News post to include one real, dated Michelin star\n    announcement (attributed to the Lomas de Chapultepec address, matching the\n    Michelin Guide's own attribution) alongside clearly labeled demo announcements, so\n    that the demo doesn't read as entirely fictional while staying honest about what's\n    invented.\n31. As anyone viewing any seed/demo content, I want a clear label distinguishing real,\n    sourced content from invented sample content, so that the site never implies La\n    Once Mil created or endorsed this project.\n32. As the grader/developer, I want two seeded demo accounts (\"Demo Manager\",\n    \"Demo Editor\") that are clearly fictional and not tied to the real owner's\n    identity, so that both roles are testable without misrepresenting real staff.\n33. As the Manager, I want the Menu page to be one ordinary Markdown page listing dish\n    names and descriptions with no prices, so that I never publish an unverified peso\n    price.\n34. As a visitor, I want image placeholders (e.g. `[PLACEHOLDER: storefront photo]`)\n    to render as clearly labeled placeholders rather than broken image tags, so that\n    missing photography doesn't look like a bug.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n- **Roles**: two enforced roles, `admin` (\"Manager\" in client-facing copy) and\n  `editor`. Role is a column on the user record, checked server-side on every\n  state-changing and every admin-only read route — never enforced only by hiding UI.\n- **Permission matrix**:\n  - `admin`: create/edit/delete/publish News; create/edit permanent pages (Home,\n    Menu, Our Story); create/edit-role/deactivate users; view dashboard and full\n    content list.\n  - `editor`: create/edit News as drafts only (cannot set status to published, cannot\n    delete a published post); cannot reach permanent-page editors or user-management\n    routes (403/redirect on both UI and direct request); can view the dashboard and\n    content list scoped to what they're allowed to act on.\n- **Content types**: `Post` (News) and `Page` (Home, Menu, Our Story) share the same\n  field set — title, slug, Markdown body, status (`draft`/`published`), author\n  (foreign key to user), created/updated timestamps. `Page` additionally participates\n  in the public navigation once published; `Post` (News) is listed reverse-chronological\n  on a News index.\n- **Pages support the same create/read/update/delete operations as Posts**\n  (corrected 2026-09-29; see the comment on this issue). Home, Menu, and Our Story\n  are La Once Mil's starting content, seeded by `scripts/seed_demo.py`, not a fixed\n  limit — the Manager can create additional Pages and delete any Page, matching the\n  manual/rubric's \"Pages: same fields and operations as posts.\" Home is not a nav\n  entry itself (it's the entry point); Menu and Our Story are.\n- **Publish/export boundary (ADR-001)**: \"published\" is a status flag in the local\n  SQLite database — it does not touch the public site. `cms publish` (via\n  `render_site()` in `app/publish.py`) reads only `status = published` rows, renders\n  Markdown through the sanitizer, and writes `site/` with one file per published item\n  plus a News index and nav links built from published Pages. `cms deploy` pushes\n  `site/` to `gh-pages`. These are three distinct moments (publish-in-CMS, export,\n  deploy) and the spec never conflates them.\n- **Sanitization**: Markdown bodies are converted with `markdown-it-py` then passed\n  through `nh3` (already a pinned dependency) before being written to `site/` or\n  rendered in the admin preview — both surfaces sanitize, not just the export.\n- **Sessions**: signed session cookies (`itsdangerous`, already pinned) carrying user\n  id and role; no client-side role trust — every request re-checks role from the\n  session-resolved user record so a deactivated user's existing session stops working\n  on the next request, not just at next login.\n- **Password storage**: argon2 (`argon2-cffi`, already pinned) hashes only; plaintext\n  is never stored or logged.\n- **CSRF**: every state-changing form (login excluded, since it has no prior session)\n  carries a CSRF token validated server-side before the action runs.\n- **Deactivation**: a boolean/status flag on the user record. Deactivated users fail\n  login with a clear rejection message even with the correct password, and any\n  existing session for that user is treated as invalid on the next authenticated\n  request.\n- **Seed data** (`scripts/seed_demo.py`): creates exactly two users, \"Demo Manager\"\n  (admin) and \"Demo Editor\" (editor), with passwords read from the environment\n  (`.env.example` documents the variable names) — never hard-coded, never the real\n  owner's name. Seeds the three permanent pages (Home/Menu/Our Story, published) and\n  a small set of News posts: one real dated item (the Michelin star announcement,\n  attributed to the Lomas de Chapultepec address) plus additional clearly-labeled\n  demo announcements (e.g. a `[DEMO CONTENT]` marker in the body or a dedicated\n  \"demo\" flag rendered as a badge).\n- **Images**: no upload/media-library feature in this spec (that's the \"Media uploads\"\n  stretch goal). Placeholder images are literal labeled text/markup\n  (`[PLACEHOLDER: storefront photo]`) embedded in seed content, rendered as visible\n  placeholder blocks, not broken `<img>` tags.\n- **Modules to build** (new, under `app/`): `app/models.py` or `app/db.py` (SQLite\n  schema: users, pages, posts), `app/auth.py` (login/session/CSRF/role-check\n  helpers), `app/routes/auth.py`, `app/routes/posts.py`, `app/routes/pages.py`,\n  `app/routes/users.py`, `app/routes/admin.py` (dashboard/content list), each\n  `include_router`'d from `app/main.py` per its existing comment. `app/publish.py`\n  extends `render_site()` to iterate published Posts/Pages instead of the current\n  hard-coded empty-items placeholder.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- Tests only assert on external behavior — HTTP status/redirect/response body for the\n  HTTP seam, and files/content under `site/` for the export seam. No test reaches into\n  the database or calls an internal function to check state a real user/visitor\n  couldn't observe.\n- **Seam 1 — HTTP** (`client`, `client_as` fixtures in `tests/conftest.py`,\n  prior art: `tests/test_t00.py`): login success/failure/logout; anonymous access to\n  any admin route redirects to login; `client_as(\"editor\")` gets 403/redirect on\n  every admin-only route (permanent-page edit, publish action, user management) —\n  including a direct POST that bypasses the UI entirely, matching the \"editors cannot\n  bypass restrictions by submitting requests directly\" requirement; Editor CRUD on\n  News is confined to draft status; Manager can publish/unpublish; dashboard and\n  content-list filters return the expected counts/subsets; user creation, role\n  change, and deactivation each work only for the admin client; a deactivated user's\n  login is refused and, if feasible to simulate, their existing session stops\n  authenticating on the next request.\n- **Seam 2 — Export** (`render_site()` in `app/publish.py`, prior art:\n  `tests/test_t00.py`'s two existing publish tests): a draft never appears in any file\n  under `site/`; a `<script>`/`onerror=` payload saved in a post's body does not\n  survive into the exported HTML unescaped/executable; every `href=`/`src=` in the\n  exported output is relative, never root-absolute; Home, Menu, Our Story, and the\n  News index are all reachable by following links present in the exported HTML\n  (no orphan published page); unpublishing an item and re-running `render_site()`\n  removes it from the output.\n- No new test seam is introduced — both existing fixtures/functions are extended, not\n  replaced.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Contact form or any customer-facing email address (phone only, per client brief).\n- A separate \"Visit Us\" page — visiting information lives entirely on Home.\n- Structured menu/pricing data or any peso prices (no verified pricing history, scheduled publishing, comments, search, categories/tags, plugin\n  system — all separate stretch goals, not required here.\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- This spec covers all 7 required Parent\n\nSpec: #1\n\n## What to build\n\nThe Manager sees a dashboard with content counts and a content list filterable by\ntype and status; the Editor sees the same dashboard scoped to what they can act on.\nEvery content editor (Pages and Posts) includes a live Markdown preview before\nsaving.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] Dashboard shows counts of News (draft vs. published) and each permanent page's\n      status.\n- [ ] Content list is filterable by type (News/Pages) and by status (draft/published).\n- [ ] Editor's content list is scoped to News only — no permanent-page rows, matching\n      their permission boundary.\n- [ ] Every content editor (Pages and Posts) includes a live Markdown preview before\n      saving.\n- [ ] `tests/test_admin_console.py` (new) covers dashboard counts, list filtering,\n      and editor-scoped list content.\n\n## Blocked by\n\n- #3 (T02: Permanent pages) — dashboard/list need Page data to summarize.\n- #5 (T04: News posts) — dashboard/list need Post data to summarize.\n\n----\n## Problem Statement\n\nLa Once Mil (the Michelin-starred flagship taquería at Monte Everest 780, Lomas de\nChapultepec, Mexico City) has no usable web presence. Its official domain\n(`laoncemil.com`) is a permanent \"under construction\" page, and its real online\npresence — Instagram, TikTok, a Linktree — buries the one thing most visitors need\n(address, hours, \"is it walk-in only\") under a scrolling feed. A first-time visitor\ndrawn by Michelin coverage, or a repeat local visitor checking today's hours, has no\nfast way to get that information without digging through a social feed they may not\nalready follow. There is also no simple way for the restaurant to post a dated\nannouncement (e.g. news, seasonal changes) without it disappearing into that same feed.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS with a local admin console (never exposed to the internet, per ADR-001)\nand a static public site exported to GitHub Pages. The public Home page leads with\nverified address, hours, a directions link, and a prominent Call button, because that\nis the one glance a visitor needs. A Manager role can publish News, edit the\npermanent pages (Home, Menu, Our Story), and manage user accounts; an Editor role can\ndraft News for the Manager to review and publish, but cannot publish anything, edit\nthe permanent pages, or touch user accounts. Publishing marks content as published in\nthe local CMS only — the public GitHub Pages site reflects that change only after\n`cms publish` exports to `site/` and that export is deployed.\n\n## User Stories\n\n1. As the Manager, I want to log in with a hashed password, so that only I can reach\n   the admin console.\n2. As the Manager, I want to log out, so that a shared or public machine doesn't stay\n   signed in.\n3. As anyone entering a wrong password, I want a clear rejection message with no\n   account created or session started, so that login failures are unambiguous.\n4. As the Editor, I want to log in with my own hashed-password account, so that I can\n   draft News without sharing the Manager's credentials.\n5. As an anonymous visitor, I want to be redirected to login when I try to reach any\n   admin route, so that the admin console is never reachable without authentication.\n6. As the Manager, I want the Editor's role to visibly differ from mine (different\n   nav options, different available actions), so that the two roles are genuinely\n   distinct, not just a label.\n7. As the Editor, I want to be blocked (403 or redirect, never a silent no-op) if I\n   submit a direct HTTP request to a Manager-only route (publish, permanent-page edit,\n   user management) even without using the UI, so that the restriction can't be\n   bypassed by hand-crafting a request.\n8. As the Manager, I want to create, edit, and delete News posts (title, slug,\n   Markdown body, draft/published status, author, timestamps), so that I can keep the\n   public site current.\n9. As the Editor, I want to create and edit News posts as drafts, so that I can\n   prepare an announcement for the Manager's review.\n10. As the Editor, I want my save actions to always leave the post in draft status\n    (never published), so that I cannot accidentally or deliberately publish content.\n11. As the Manager, I want to publish or unpublish a News post, so that I control what\n    reaches the public site.\n12. As the Manager, I want the News list to be filterable by status (draft/published),\n    so that I can find what still needs review.\n13. As the Manager, I want to edit the Home, Menu, and Our Story pages (each with\n    title, slug, Markdown body, draft/published status, author, timestamps, matching\n    the Posts capability's fields), so that I control the site's permanent content.\n14. As the Editor, I want the permanent-page editors to be unreachable to me (no UI\n    entry point, and a 403/redirect if I try the route directly), so that the\n    \"Editor never touches permanent pages\" rule is enforced, not just hidden.\n15. As a visitor, I want a published permanent page to appear in the public site's\n    navigation once it's exported, so that I can reach Menu and Our Story from Home.\n16. As the Manager, I want a dashboard that shows counts of News posts (draft vs.\n    published) and permanent-page status, so that I can see the site's state at a\n    glance.\n17. As the Manager, I want a content list I can filter by type (News/Pages) and by\n    status, so that I can navigate a growing set of content without scrolling\n    everything.\n18. As the Manager or Editor, I want a Markdown preview in the editor before saving,\n    so that I can see how the rendered content will look.\n19. As the Manager, I want to create a new user account (email, password, role), so\n    that I can add an Editor when needed.\n20. As the Manager, I want to change a user's role, so that I can promote or demote\n    without deleting and recreating the account.\n21. As the Manager, I want to deactivate a user, so that someone who shouldn't have\n    access anymore loses it without deleting their historical authorship record.\n22. As a deactivated user, I want my login attempt to be refused with a clear message,\n    even with the correct password, so that deactivation is actually enforced.\n23. As the Editor, I want the user-management routes to be unreachable to me (no UI\n    entry point, and a 403/redirect on direct request), so that only the Manager\n    controls accounts.\n24. As a visitor, I want the public Home page to show verified address, hours, a\n    directions link, and a prominent Call button, prioritized above other content\n    (signature dish, walk-in-only note), so that I get the \"where and when\" answer\n    immediately.\n25. As a visitor, I want the public site to show only published content — no draft\n    ever appears — so that I never see unfinished or unreviewed material.\n26. As a visitor, I want any Markdown-authored content (News, Home, Menu, Our Story)\n    to render safely, with scripts and dangerous attributes stripped, so that the\n    public site can't be used to inject malicious content even if a draft author\n    pasted something unsafe.\n27. As a visitor, I want every link and asset on the exported site to use a relative\n    path (never root-absolute), so that the site works correctly when served from a\n    GitHub Pages project subfolder.\n28. As a visitor, I want every published page — including Home — reachable by\n    clicking through the site's navigation, so that no published content is an\n    unlinked orphan.\n29. As the Manager, I want `cms publish` to regenerate `site/` from only the current\n    published content, so that unpublishing something and re-running publish actually\n    removes it from the export.\n30. As the Manager, I want a News post to include one real, dated Michelin star\n    announcement (attributed to the Lomas de Chapultepec address, matching the\n    Michelin Guide's own attribution) alongside clearly labeled demo announcements, so\n    that the demo doesn't read as entirely fictional while staying honest about what's\n    invented.\n31. As anyone viewing any seed/demo content, I want a clear label distinguishing real,\n    sourced content from invented sample content, so that the site never implies La\n    Once Mil created or endorsed this project.\n32. As the grader/developer, I want two seeded demo accounts (\"Demo Manager\",\n    \"Demo Editor\") that are clearly fictional and not tied to the real owner's\n    identity, so that both roles are testable without misrepresenting real staff.\n33. As the Manager, I want the Menu page to be one ordinary Markdown page listing dish\n    names and descriptions with no prices, so that I never publish an unverified peso\n    price.\n34. As a visitor, I want image placeholders (e.g. `[PLACEHOLDER: storefront photo]`)\n    to render as clearly labeled placeholders rather than broken image tags, so that\n    missing photography doesn't look like a bug.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n- **Roles**: two enforced roles, `admin` (\"Manager\" in client-facing copy) and\n  `editor`. Role is a column on the user record, checked server-side on every\n  state-changing and every admin-only read route — never enforced only by hiding UI.\n- **Permission matrix**:\n  - `admin`: create/edit/delete/publish News; create/edit permanent pages (Home,\n    Menu, Our Story); create/edit-role/deactivate users; view dashboard and full\n    content list.\n  - `editor`: create/edit News as drafts only (cannot set status to published, cannot\n    delete a published post); cannot reach permanent-page editors or user-management\n    routes (403/redirect on both UI and direct request); can view the dashboard and\n    content list scoped to what they're allowed to act on.\n- **Content types**: `Post` (News) and `Page` (Home, Menu, Our Story) share the same\n  field set — title, slug, Markdown body, status (`draft`/`published`), author\n  (foreign key to user), created/updated timestamps. `Page` additionally participates\n  in the public navigation once published; `Post` (News) is listed reverse-chronological\n  on a News index.\n- **Pages support the same create/read/update/delete operations as Posts**\n  (corrected 2026-09-29; see the comment on this issue). Home, Menu, and Our Story\n  are La Once Mil's starting content, seeded by `scripts/seed_demo.py`, not a fixed\n  limit — the Manager can create additional Pages and delete any Page, matching the\n  manual/rubric's \"Pages: same fields and operations as posts.\" Home is not a nav\n  entry itself (it's the entry point); Menu and Our Story are.\n- **Publish/export boundary (ADR-001)**: \"published\" is a status flag in the local\n  SQLite database — it does not touch the public site. `cms publish` (via\n  `render_site()` in `app/publish.py`) reads only `status = published` rows, renders\n  Markdown through the sanitizer, and writes `site/` with one file per published item\n  plus a News index and nav links built from published Pages. `cms deploy` pushes\n  `site/` to `gh-pages`. These are three distinct moments (publish-in-CMS, export,\n  deploy) and the spec never conflates them.\n- **Sanitization**: Markdown bodies are converted with `markdown-it-py` then passed\n  through `nh3` (already a pinned dependency) before being written to `site/` or\n  rendered in the admin preview — both surfaces sanitize, not just the export.\n- **Sessions**: signed session cookies (`itsdangerous`, already pinned) carrying user\n  id and role; no client-side role trust — every request re-checks role from the\n  session-resolved user record so a deactivated user's existing session stops working\n  on the next request, not just at next login.\n- **Password storage**: argon2 (`argon2-cffi`, already pinned) hashes only; plaintext\n  is never stored or logged.\n- **CSRF**: every state-changing form (login excluded, since it has no prior session)\n  carries a CSRF token validated server-side before the action runs.\n- **Deactivation**: a boolean/status flag on the user record. Deactivated users fail\n  login with a clear rejection message even with the correct password, and any\n  existing session for that user is treated as invalid on the next authenticated\n  request.\n- **Seed data** (`scripts/seed_demo.py`): creates exactly two users, \"Demo Manager\"\n  (admin) and \"Demo Editor\" (editor), with passwords read from the environment\n  (`.env.example` documents the variable names) — never hard-coded, never the real\n  owner's name. Seeds the three permanent pages (Home/Menu/Our Story, published) and\n  a small set of News posts: one real dated item (the Michelin star announcement,\n  attributed to the Lomas de Chapultepec address) plus additional clearly-labeled\n  demo announcements (e.g. a `[DEMO CONTENT]` marker in the body or a dedicated\n  \"demo\" flag rendered as a badge).\n- **Images**: no upload/media-library feature in this spec (that's the \"Media uploads\"\n  stretch goal). Placeholder images are literal labeled text/markup\n  (`[PLACEHOLDER: storefront photo]`) embedded in seed content, rendered as visible\n  placeholder blocks, not broken `<img>` tags.\n- **Modules to build** (new, under `app/`): `app/models.py` or `app/db.py` (SQLite\n  schema: users, pages, posts), `app/auth.py` (login/session/CSRF/role-check\n  helpers), `app/routes/auth.py`, `app/routes/posts.py`, `app/routes/pages.py`,\n  `app/routes/users.py`, `app/routes/admin.py` (dashboard/content list), each\n  `include_router`'d from `app/main.py` per its existing comment. `app/publish.py`\n  extends `render_site()` to iterate published Posts/Pages instead of the current\n  hard-coded empty-items placeholder.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- Tests only assert on external behavior — HTTP status/redirect/response body for the\n  HTTP seam, and files/content under `site/` for the export seam. Only the Manager publishes, unpublishes, or\n    deletes published posts; Editors are refused (403) on a published post by UI\n    and by direct request. cms publish exports news.html (newest publish date\n    first, linked from the nav) and news-<slug>.html for published posts only;\n    unpublishing and re-exporting removes the old file.\n    \n    \"news\" and the \"news-\" prefix are reserved for News: Pages cannot use them,\n    existing conflicting Pages are reported (never deleted), and the export\n    refuses to run, leaving the previous site/ intact. \"new\" is reserved so no\n    item shadows /admin/*/new. Auth checks share auth.require_role(). Seed adds\n    the dated Michelin item plus [DEMO CONTENT] posts.\n    \n    Code-review findings and their resolution, and test results, are posted on #5.\n    110 tests pass.\n    \n    Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\n    Closes #5\n\n app/auth.py                      |  17 +-\n app/db.py                        | 137 +++++++++++++-\n app/main.py                      |   7 +-\n app/publish.py                   |  70 +++++--\n app/routes/pages.py              |  27 ++-\n app/routes/posts.py              | 201 ++++++++++++++++++++\n scripts/seed_demo.py             |  47 ++++-\n templates/admin/dashboard.html   |   1 +\n templates/admin/page_edit.html   |   1 +\n templates/admin/pages_list.html  |   8 +\n templates/admin/post_edit.html   |  30 +++\n templates/admin/post_new.html    |  15 ++\n templates/admin/posts_list.html  |  24 +++\n templates/public/news_index.html |  14 ++\n tests/test_posts.py              | 387 +++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++\napp/auth.py\napp/db.py\napp/__init__.py\napp/content.py\napp/cli.py\napp/settings.py\napp/main.py\napp/urls.py\napp/publish.py\ntemplates/base.html\ntests/conftest.py\ntests/test_pages.py\ntests/test_login.py\ntests/test_admin_links.py\ntests/test_admin_shell.py\ntests/test_usage_report.py\ntests/test_ctx_guard.py\ntests/test_t00.py\ntests/test_publish.py\ntests/test_posts.py\napp/routes/auth.py\napp/routes/posts.py\napp/routes/__init__.py\napp/routes/pages.py\ntemplates/admin/pages_list.html\ntemplates/admin/posts_list.html\ntemplates/admin/login.html\ntemplates/admin/hello.html\ntemplates/admin/dashboard.html\ntemplates/admin/post_new.html\ntemplates/admin/page_edit.html\ntemplates/admin/base_admin.html\ntemplates/admin/post_edit.html\ntemplates/admin/page_new.html\ntemplates/public/page.html\ntemplates/public/home.html\ntemplates/public/news_index.html\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nT00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\nanswers at /. That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import Response\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import publish, settings, urls\n\ntemplates = urls.install(Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES)))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin/style.css\")\n    def admin_stylesheet():\n        # Same stylesheet the exported site ships; admin pages link it relatively.\n        return Response(publish.CSS, media_type=\"text/css\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    from app.routes import auth, pages, posts\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(pages.router)\n    app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n       6 app/__init__.py\n     119 app/auth.py\n      56 app/cli.py\n      34 app/content.py\n     362 app/db.py\n      49 app/main.py\n     117 app/publish.py\n      16 app/settings.py\n      30 app/urls.py\n       0 app/routes/__init__.py\n      75 app/routes/auth.py\n     173 app/routes/pages.py\n     201 app/routes/posts.py\n    1238 total","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"d03197eb-f01c-40a9-ac59-79031b542058","timestamp":"2026-10-13T03:22:58.926Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"commit 88e7480085d0b594aeac7ee385f0c14aa49ea282\nAuthor: Alejandro González <algoal0507@gmail.com>\nDate:   Mon Oct 12 23:18:48 2026 -0400\n\n    T04: News posts with Editor drafts and Manager publish\n    \n    Editors create and edit News as drafts; cat templates/admin/{dashboard,page_edit,post_edit,page_new,post_new,posts_list,pages_list,base_admin}.html; cat tests/conftest.py; grep -n \"^def \\|^class \" app/db.py"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_011YAB4C1p1ghYTNuQYizY1x":{"cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"serverClassifierRequest":"da2abece-caee-452b-8dec-3239b5461cb0","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfyX4thgBxkiMP2g2NAjd","requestedModel":"claude-sonnet-5-5","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:implement","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"c882e08c-c6be-4706-a326-75afeb66c817","timestamp":"2026-10-13T03:23:00.730Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"68fe07ff-a133-4d44-8ff0-716e95c03e55","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/alejandrogonzalez/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"b36ba01a-82f5-45f0-a1a0-30321be8bf78","version":"2.1.296","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"c882e08c-c6be-4706-a326-75afeb66c817","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"bf41cbd0-9b6f-4cb6-b4f0-9851f6e5a70f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011YAB4C1p1ghYTNuQYizY1x","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Password hashing, signed session cookies, and CSRF tokens.\n\nSession: a signed, timed token (itsdangerous) carrying the user id, stored in\nan httponly cookie. Nothing about the session lives server-side, so there is\nno session table to clean up.\n\nCSRF: the double-submit cookie pattern. `new_csrf_token` + `set_csrf_cookie`\nput a random value in a cookie *and* a hidden form field; `csrf_is_valid`\nchecks the two match. An attacker forging a cross-site POST cannot read the\ncookie, so cannot supply a matching field.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHash, VerificationError, VerifyMismatchError\nfrom itsdangerous import BadSignature, SignatureExpired, URLSafeTimedSerializer\nfrom starlette.requests import Request\nfrom starlette.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse, Response\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nSESSION_COOKIE = \"session\"\nSESSION_MAX_AGE = 60 * 60 * 8  # 8 hours\nCSRF_COOKIE = \"csrf_token\"\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n_serializer = URLSafeTimedSerializer(settings.SECRET_KEY, salt=\"session\")\n\n\ndef hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(password)\n\n\ndef verify_password(password: str, password_hash: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return _hasher.verify(password_hash, password)\n    except (VerifyMismatchError, VerificationError, InvalidHash):\n        return False\n\n\ndef set_session_cookie(response: Response, user_id: int) -> None:\n    token = _serializer.dumps({\"user_id\": user_id})\n    response.set_cookie(\n        SESSION_COOKIE, token, max_age=SESSION_MAX_AGE,\n        httponly=True, samesite=\"lax\",\n    )\n\n\ndef clear_session_cookie(response: Response) -> None:\n    response.delete_cookie(SESSION_COOKIE)\n\n\ndef read_session(request: Request) -> int | None:\n    token = request.cookies.get(SESSION_COOKIE)\n    if not token:\n        return None\n    try:\n        data = _serializer.loads(token, max_age=SESSION_MAX_AGE)\n    except (BadSignature, SignatureExpired):\n        return None\n    return data.get(\"user_id\")\n\n\ndef new_csrf_token() -> str:\n    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef set_csrf_cookie(response: Response, token: str) -> None:\n    response.set_cookie(CSRF_COOKIE, token, httponly=True, samesite=\"lax\")\n\n\ndef csrf_is_valid(request: Request, submitted: str | None) -> bool:\n    cookie_value = request.cookies.get(CSRF_COOKIE)\n    if not cookie_value or not submitted:\n        return False\n    return secrets.compare_digest(cookie_value, submitted)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"The active, logged-in user for this request, or None.\n\n    Imports app.db lazily — app.db imports this module at load time, so a\n    top-level import here would be circular.\n    \"\"\"\n    from app import db\n\n    user_id = read_session(request)\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = db.get_user_by_id(user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef render_with_csrf(templates, request: Request, template_name: str, context: dict, *, status_code: int = 200):\n    token = new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, template_name, {**context, \"csrf_token\": token}, status_code=status_code\n    )\n    set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\ndef require_role(request: Request, *roles: str):\n    \"\"\"Gate a route: (user, None) if allowed, else (None, response).\n\n    No roles means any logged-in user. An anonymous caller is redirected to\n    /login; a logged-in caller with the wrong role gets a 403. Every admin\n    route calls this, so the rule lives in one place.\n    \"\"\"\n    user = current_user(request)\n    if user is None:\n        return None, RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    if roles and user[\"role\"] not in roles:\n        return None, PlainTextResponse(\"Forbidden.\", status_code=403)\n    return user, None\n\"\"\"Markdown rendering and HTML sanitization for user-written content.\n\n`_md` is built with html=False, so raw HTML in Markdown source is always\nescaped as literal text on render — that is the actual security boundary,\nnot string-scrubbing. `sanitize_markdown_source` is defense in depth on top\nof that: it strips <script>/<style> blocks and on* event attributes from the\nstored source with plain regexes rather than an HTML sanitizer, because\nrunning Markdown source through an HTML sanitizer (e.g. nh3.clean) HTML-\nentity-encodes ordinary characters like \"&\" — corrupting the text on every\nsave/reload round trip instead of just neutralizing danger.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport nh3\nfrom markdown_it import MarkdownIt\n\n_md = MarkdownIt(\"commonmark\", {\"html\": False})\n\n_SCRIPT_RE = re.compile(r\"<script\\b[^>]*>.*?</script\\s*>\", re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)\n_STYLE_RE = re.compile(r\"<style\\b[^>]*>.*?</style\\s*>\", re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)\n_EVENT_ATTR_RE = re.compile(r\"\"\"\\son\\w+\\s*=\\s*(\"[^\"]*\"|'[^']*'|[^\\s>]+)\"\"\", re.IGNORECASE)\n\n\ndef sanitize_markdown_source(text: str) -> str:\n    text = _SCRIPT_RE.sub(\"\", text)\n    text = _STYLE_RE.sub(\"\", text)\n    text = _EVENT_ATTR_RE.sub(\"\", text)\n    return text\n\n\ndef render_markdown(text: str) -> str:\n    return nh3.clean(_md.render(text))\n\"\"\"Page editing: create, read, update, delete.\n\nManager (admin) only — the Editor must be blocked in code, not just by hiding\nthe link, on every route here, including a hand-crafted POST. Home, Menu, and\nOur Story are La Once Mil's starting content (see db.PAGE_SLUGS), not a limit:\nper the MP2 rubric/manual, Pages support the same create/read/update/delete\noperations as Posts.\n\nRoute order matters: /admin/pages/new must be declared before the\n/admin/pages/{slug} routes, or FastAPI would match \"new\" as a slug.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, content, db, _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        login_page = c.get(\"/login\")\n        if login_page.status_code == 404:\n user[\"password\"],\n            \"csrf_token\": login_page.cookies.get(\"csrf_token\"),\n        }, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n tokens.\n\nSession: a signed, timed token (itsdangerous) carrying the user id, stored in\nan httponly cookie. Nothing about the session lives server-side, so there is\nno session table to clean up.\n\nCSRF: the double-submit cookie pattern. `new_csrf_token` + `set_csrf_cookie`\nput a random value in a cookie *and* a hidden form field; `csrf_is_valid`\nchecks the two match. An attacker forging a cross-site POST cannot read the\ncookie, so cannot supply a matching field.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHash, VerificationError, VerifyMismatchError\nfrom itsdangerous import BadSignature, SignatureExpired, URLSafeTimedSerializer\nfrom starlette.requests import Request\nfrom starlette.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse, Response\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nSESSION_COOKIE = \"session\"\nSESSION_MAX_AGE = 60 * 60 * 8  # 8 hours\nCSRF_COOKIE = \"csrf_token\"\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n_serializer = URLSafeTimedSerializer(settings.SECRET_KEY, salt=\"session\")\n\n\ndef hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(password)\n\n\ndef verify_password(password: str, password_hash: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return _hasher.verify(password_hash, password)\n    except (VerifyMismatchError, VerificationError, InvalidHash):\n        return False\n\n\ndef set_session_cookie(response: Response, user_id: int) -> None:\n    token = _serializer.dumps({\"user_id\": user_id})\n    response.set_cookie(\n        SESSION_COOKIE, token, max_age=SESSION_MAX_AGE,\n        httponly=True, samesite=\"lax\",\n    )\n\n\ndef clear_session_cookie(response: Response) -> None:\n    response.delete_cookie(SESSION_COOKIE)\n\n\ndef read_session(request: Request) -> int | None:\n    token = request.cookies.get(SESSION_COOKIE)\n    if not token:\n        return None\n    try:\n        data = _serializer.loads(token, max_age=SESSION_MAX_AGE)\n    except (BadSignature, SignatureExpired):\n        return None\n    return data.get(\"user_id\")\n\n\ndef new_csrf_token() -> str:\n    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef set_csrf_cookie(response: Response, token: str) -> None:\n    response.set_cookie(CSRF_COOKIE, token, httponly=True, samesite=\"lax\")\n\n\ndef csrf_is_valid(request: Request, submitted: str | None) -> bool:\n    cookie_value = request.cookies.get(CSRF_COOKIE)\n    if not cookie_value or not submitted:\n        return False\n    return secrets.compare_digest(cookie_value, submitted)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"The active, logged-in user for this request, or None.\n\n    Imports app.db lazily — app.db imports this module at load time, so a\n    top-level import here would be circular.\n    \"\"\"\n    from app import db\n\n    user_id = read_session(request)\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = db.get_user_by_id(user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef render_with_csrf(templates, request: Request, template_name: str, context: dict, *, status_code: int = 200):\n    token = new_csrf_token()\n    response = templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, template_name, {**context, \"csrf_token\": token}, status_code=status_code\n    )\n    set_csrf_cookie(response, token)\n    return response\n\n\ndef require_role(request: Request, *roles: str):\n    \"\"\"Gate a route: (user, None) if allowed, else (None, response).\n\n    No roles means any logged-in user. An anonymous caller is redirected to\n    /login; a logged-in caller with the wrong role gets a 403. Every admin\n    route calls this, so the rule lives in one place.\n    \"\"\"\n    user = current_user(request)\n    if user is None:\n        return None, RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    if roles and user[\"role\"] not in roles:\n        return None, PlainTextResponse(\"Forbidden.\", status_code=403)\n    return user, None\n\"\"\"Markdown rendering and HTML sanitization for user-written content.\n\n`_md` is built with html=False, so raw HTML in Markdown source is always\nescaped as literal text on render — that is the actual security boundary,\nnot string-scrubbing. `sanitize_markdown_source` is defense in depth on top\nof that: it strips <script>/<style> blocks and on* event attributes from the\nstored source with plain regexes rather than an HTML sanitizer, because\nrunning Markdown source through an HTML sanitizer (e.g. nh3.clean) HTML-\nentity-encodes ordinary characters like \"&\" — corrupting the text on every\nsave/reload round trip instead of just neutralizing danger.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport nh3\nfrom markdown_it import MarkdownIt\n\n_md = MarkdownIt(\"commonmark\", {\"html\": False})\n\n_SCRIPT_RE = re.compile(r\"<script\\b[^>]*>.*?</script\\s*>\", re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)\n_STYLE_RE = re.compile(r\"<style\\b[^>]*>.*?</style\\s*>\", re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)\n_EVENT_ATTR_RE = re.compile(r\"\"\"\\son\\w+\\s*=\\s*(\"[^\"]*\"|'[^']*'|[^\\s>]+)\"\"\", re.IGNORECASE)\n\n\ndef sanitize_markdown_source(text: str) -> str:\n    text = _SCRIPT_RE.sub(\"\", text)\n    text = _STYLE_RE.sub(\"\", text)\n    text = _EVENT_ATTR_RE.sub(\"\", text)\n    return text\n\n\ndef render_markdown(text: str) -> str:\n    return nh3.clean(_md.render(text))\n\"\"\"Page editing: create, read, update, delete.\n\nManager (admin) only — the Editor must be blocked in code, not just by hiding\nthe link, on every route here, including a hand-crafted POST. Home, Menu, and\nOur Story are La Once Mil's starting content (see db.PAGE_SLUGS), not a limit:\nper the MP2 rubric/manual, Pages support the same create/read/update/delete\noperations as Posts.\n\nRoute order matters: /admin/pages/new must be declared before the\n/admin/pages/{slug} routes, or FastAPI would match \"new\" as a slug.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, content, db, settings, urls\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\ntemplates = urls.install(Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES)))\n\n\ndef _require_manager(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"(user, None) if the caller may manage pages, else (None, response).\"\"\"\n    return auth.require_role(request, \"admin\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/pages\")\ndef pages_list(request: Request):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n        templates, request, \"admin/pages_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Pages\", \"pages\": db.list_pages(),\n         \"news_conflicts\": db.pages_conflicting_with_news()},\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/pages/new\")\ndef page_new_form(request: Request):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n        templates, request, \"admin/page_new.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"New page\", \"error\": None,\n         \"values\": {\"slug\": \"\", \"title\": \"\", \"body_md\": \"\", \"status\": \"draft\"}},\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/pages/new\")\ndef page_new_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    slug: str = Form(...),\n    title: str = Form(...),\n    body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n    status: str = Form(\"draft\"),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n    if status not in (\"draft\", \"published\"):\n        status = \"draft\"\n\n    values = {\"slug\": slug, \"title\": title, \"body_md\": body_md, \"status\": status}\n\n    if not db.slug_is_valid(slug):\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, \"admin/page_new.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"New page\", \"values\": values,\n             \"error\": \"Slug must be lowercase letters, numbers, and hyphens only.\"},\n            status_code=400,\n        )\n    if db.is_news_slug(slug):\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, \"admin/page_new.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"New page\", \"values\": values,\n             \"error\": 'The slug \"news\" and slugs starting with \"news-\" are reserved for News.'},\n            status_code=400,\n        )\n\n    created = db.create_page(\n        slug, title=title,\n        body_md=content.sanitize_markdown_source(body_md),\n        status=status, author_id=user[\"id\"],\n    )\n    if not created:\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, \"admin/page_new.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"New page\", \"values\": values,\n             \"error\": f'A page with the slug \"{slug}\" already exists.'},\n            status_code=400,\n        )\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{slug}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/pages/{slug}\")\ndef page_edit_form(request: Request, slug: str):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    page = db.get_page_by_slug(slug)\n    if page is None:\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Not found.\", status_code=404)\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n        templates, request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": f\"Edit {page['title']}\", \"page\": page,\n         \"preview_html\": content.render_markdown(page[\"body_md\"]), \"saved\": False},\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/pages/{slug}\")\ndef page_edit_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    slug: str,\n    title: str = Form(...),\n    body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n    status: str = Form(\"draft\"),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    if db.get_page_by_slug(slug) is None:\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Not found.\", status_code=404)\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n    if status not in (\"draft\", \"published\"):\n        status = \"draft\"\n    if status == \"published\" and db.is_news_slug(slug):\n        page = db.get_page_by_slug(slug)\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n            {\"title\": f\"Edit {page['title']}\", \"page\": page,\n             \"preview_html\": content.render_markdown(page[\"body_md\"]), \"saved\": False,\n             \"error\": f'\"{slug}\" is in the namespace reserved for News, so this page '\n                      \"cannot be published. Create a page with a different slug instead.\"},\n            status_code=400,\n        )\n\n    db.update_page(\n        slug,\n        title=title,\n        body_md=content.sanitize_markdown_source(body_md),\n        status=status,\n        author_id=user[\"id\"],\n    )\n\n    page = db.get_page_by_slug(slug)\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n        templates, request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": f\"Edit {page['title']}\", \"page\": page,\n         \"preview_html\": content.render_markdown(page[\"body_md\"]), \"saved\": True},\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/pages/{slug}/delete\")\ndef page_delete(request: Request, slug: str, csrf_token: str | None = Form(None)):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    if db.get_page_by_slug(slug) is None:\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Not found.\", status_code=404)\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n\n    db.delete_page(slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/pages\", status_code=303)\n\"\"\"News posts: create, read, update, publish, delete.\n\nEditor and Manager both reach these routes, but what they may do differs and\nis enforced here in code, never by hiding links. A post's status is never read\nfrom a form field: new and edited posts keep their current status, and only\nthe Manager-only publish/unpublish routes change it, so a hand-crafted POST\ncannot publish anything.\n\nRoute order matters: /admin/posts/new must be declared before the\n/admin/posts/{slug} routes, or FastAPI would match \"new\" as a slug.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, content, db, settings, urls\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\ntemplates = urls.install(Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES)))\n\n\ndef _require_staff(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"(user, None) for any logged-in Editor or Manager, else (None, response).\"\"\"\n    return auth.require_role(request)\n\n\ndef _require_manager(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"(user, None) if the caller is the Manager, else (None, response).\"\"\"\n    return auth.require_role(request, \"admin\")\n\n\ndef _editor_locked_out(user, post) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"An Editor may only touch drafts; a published post is Manager-only.\"\"\"\n    return user[\"role\"] != \"admin\" and post[\"status\"] == \"published\"\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/posts\")\ndef posts_list(request: Request):\n    user, error = post\", \"error\": None,\n         \"values\": {\"slug\": \"\", \"title\": \"\", \"body_md\": \"\"}},\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/posts/new\")\ndef post_new_submit(\n    request: Request,\n None:\n        return error\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n\n    values = {\"slug\": slug, \"title\": title, \"body_md\": body_md}\n    if not db.slug_is_valid(slug):\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, \"admin/post_new.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"New News status_code=400,\n        )\n    if not title.strip():\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, \"admin/post_new.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"New News post\", body_md=content.sanitize_markdown_source(body_md),\n        author_id=user[\"id\"],\n    )\n    if not created:\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, status_code=400,\n        )\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{slug}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/posts/{slug}\")\ndef post_edit_form(request: Request, slug: str):\n    user, error = _require_staff(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    post = db.get_post_by_slug(slug)\n    if post is None:\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Not found.\", status_code=404)\n    if _editor_locked_out(user, post):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Forbidden.\", status_code=403)\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(\n        templates, request, \"admin/post_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": f\"Edit {post['title']}\", \"post\": post, \"is_manager\": user[\"role\"] == \"admin\",\n         \"preview_html\": content.render_markdown(post[\"body_md\"]), \"saved\": False},\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/posts/{slug}\")\ndef post_edit_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    slug: str,\n    title: str = Form(...),\n    body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    user, error = _require_staff(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    existing = title.strip():\n        return auth.render_with_csrf(\n            templates, request, \"admin/post_edit.html\",\n            {\"title\": f\"Edit {existing['title']}\", \"post\": existing,\n csrf_token: str | None, status: str):\n    user, error = _require_manager(request)\n    if error is not None:\n        return error\n    if db.get_post_by_slug(slug) is None:\n        return db.set_post_status(slug, status)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{slug}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/posts/{slug}/publish\")\ndef post_publish(request: Request, slug: Request, slug: str, csrf_token: str | None = Form(None)):\n    return _change_status(request, slug, csrf_token, \"draft\")\n\n\n@router.post(\"/admin/posts/{slug}/delete\")\ndef post_delete(request: auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n\n    db.delete_post(slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n{% extends \"admin/base_admin.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Welcome, {{ role_label }}</h1>\n  <p>Signed in as {{ email }}.</p>\n  {% if role_label == \"Manager\" %}\n (Markdown)\n      <textarea name=\"body_md\" rows=\"16\" cols=\"80\">{{ post['body_md'] }}</textarea>\n    </label>\n    <button type=\"submit\">Save</button>\n  </form>\n  {% if is_manager %}\n    {% if post['status'] == 'draft' %}\n      <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post['slug'] }}/publish\">\n        <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n        <button type=\"submit\">Publish</button>\n      </form>\n    {% else %}\n      <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post['slug'] }}/unpublish\">\n        <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n        <button type=\"submit\">Unpublish</button>\n      </form>\n    {% endif %}\n  {% endif %}\n  <h2>Preview</h2>\n  <div class=\"preview\">{{ preview_html | safe }}</div>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"admin/base_admin.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>New page</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p class=\"error\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/pages/new\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <label>Slug <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ values['slug'] {"parentUuid":"9be325e8-779a-4713-bdb1-c6b9c27c7718","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"bf41cbd0-9b6f-4cb6-b4f0-9851f6e5a70f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01B99UEVPsd3FiEzidpnuZZZ","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"Login, logout, and the one protected landing route T01 needs.\n\nLater tickets add real admin routes; they should follow the same pattern —\n`auth.read_session` to find the user, redirect to /login if there isn't one.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings, urls\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\ntemplates = urls.install(Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES)))\n\n\n_current_user = auth.current_user\n\n\ndef _render_with_csrf(request: Request, template_name: str, context: dict, *, status_code: int = 200):\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(templates, request, template_name, context, status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    if _current_user(request) is not None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    return _render_with_csrf(request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"error\": None})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\")\ndef login_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    email: str = Form(...),\n    password: str = Form(...),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Your session expired. Please try again.\", status_code=403)\n\n    user = db.get_user_by_email(email)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"] or not auth.verify_password(password, user[\"password_hash\"]):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Invalid email or password.\", status_code=401)\n\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    auth.set_session_cookie(response, user[\"id\"])\n    return response\n\n\ndef _rejected(request: Request, message: str, *, status_code: int):\n    return _render_with_csrf(\n        request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"error\": message}, status_code=status_code\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\")\ndef logout(request: Request, csrf_token: str | None = Form(None)):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    auth.clear_session_cookie(response)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/dashboard\")\ndef admin_dashboard(request: Request):\n    user = _current_user(request)\n    if user is None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    role_label = \"Manager\" if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\" else \"Editor\"\n    return _render_with_csrf(\n        request, \"admin/dashboard.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Dashboard\", \"role_label\": role_label, \"email\": user[\"email\"]},\n    )\n\"\"\"Relative URLs for admin pages, which are served at nested routes.\n\nThe exported public site is flat, so fixed names like \"style.css\" work there.\nAdmin routes nest (/login, /admin/pages/home), so a link is computed from the\nrequest's own path, the way a browser will resolve it: relative to the\n*directory* of the current URL.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport posixpath\n\nfrom jinja2 import pass_context\nfrom starlette.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\n\ndef relative_url(from_path: str, to_path: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The relative reference that, resolved from `from_path`, yields `to_path`.\"\"\"\n    base = from_path if from_path.endswith(\"/\") else posixpath.dirname(from_path)\n    return posixpath.relpath(to_path, base or \"/\")\n\n\n@pass_context\ndef _rel(context, to_path: str) -> str:\n    return relative_url(context[\"request\"].url.path, to_path)\n\n\ndef install(templates: Jinja2Templates) -> Jinja2Templates:\n    \"\"\"Expose `rel('/admin/dashboard')` to templates rendered with a request.\"\"\"\n    templates.env.globals[\"rel\"] = _rel\n    return templates\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_page_by_slug(slug: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT * FROM pages WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)\n        ).fetchone()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef list_pages() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages ORDER BY slug\").fetchall()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef update_page(slug: str, *, title: str, body_md: str, status: str, author_id: int) -> None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"UPDATE pages\n               SET title = ?, body_md = ?, status = ?, author_id = ?,\n                   updated_at = datetime('now')\n               WHERE slug = ?\"\"\",\n            (title, body_md, status, author_id, slug),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef slug_is_valid(slug: str) -> bool:\n    return slug not in _RESERVED_SLUGS and re.fullmatch(SLUG_PATTERN, slug) is not None\n\n\n# News owns these export filenames: news.html is the index and\n# news-<slug>.html is each post. A Page slugged \"news\" or \"news-…\" would write\n# to the same file, so Pages may not use them.\nNEWS_INDEX_SLUG = \"news\"\nNEWS_PREFIX = \"news-\"\n\n\ndef is_news_slug(slug: str) -> bool:\n    return slug == NEWS_INDEX_SLUG or slug.startswith(NEWS_PREFIX)\n\n\ndef pages_conflicting_with_news() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"Existing Pages whose slug now falls in the News namespace. Reported\n    only; nothing is renamed or deleted.\"\"\"\n    return [p for p in list_pages() if is_news_slug(p[\"slug\"])]\n\n\ndef create_page(slug: str, *, title: str, body_md: str, status: str, author_id: int) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Insert a new page. Returns False if the slug is already taken.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO pages (slug, title, body_md, status, author_id) VALUES (?, ?, logout, and the one protected landing route T01 needs.\n\nLater tickets add real admin routes; they should follow the same pattern —\n`auth.read_session` to find the user, redirect to /login if there isn't one.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import PlainTextResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings, urls\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\ntemplates = urls.install(Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES)))\n\n\n_current_user = auth.current_user\n\n\ndef _render_with_csrf(request: Request, template_name: str, context: dict, *, status_code: int = 200):\n    return auth.render_with_csrf(templates, request, template_name, context, status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    if _current_user(request) is not None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    return _render_with_csrf(request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"error\": None})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\")\ndef login_submit(\n    request: Request,\n    email: str = Form(...),\n    password: str = Form(...),\n    csrf_token: str | None = Form(None),\n):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Your session expired. Please try again.\", status_code=403)\n\n    user = db.get_user_by_email(email)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"] or not auth.verify_password(password, user[\"password_hash\"]):\n        return _rejected(request, \"Invalid email or password.\", status_code=401)\n\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/admin/dashboard\", status_code=303)\n    auth.set_session_cookie(response, user[\"id\"])\n    return response\n\n\ndef _rejected(request: Request, message: str, *, status_code: int):\n    return _render_with_csrf(\n        request, \"admin/login.html\", {\"title\": \"Log in\", \"error\": message}, status_code=status_code\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\")\ndef logout(request: Request, csrf_token: str | None = Form(None)):\n    if not auth.csrf_is_valid(request, csrf_token):\n        return PlainTextResponse(\"Invalid CSRF token.\", status_code=403)\n    response = RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    auth.clear_session_cookie(response)\n    return response\n\n\n@router.get(\"/admin/dashboard\")\ndef admin_dashboard(request: Request):\n    user = _current_user(request)\n    if user is None:\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n    role_label = \"Manager\" if user[\"role\"] == \"admin\" else \"Editor\"\n    return _render_with_csrf(\n        request, \"admin/dashboard.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Dashboard\", \"role_label\": role_label, \"email\": user[\"email\"]},\n    )\n\"\"\"Relative URLs for admin pages, which are served at nested routes.\n\nThe exported public site is flat, so fixed names like \"style.css\" work there.\nAdmin routes nest (/login, /admin/pages/home), so a link is computed from the\nrequest's own path, the way a browser will resolve it: relative to the\n*directory* of the current URL.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport posixpath\n\nfrom jinja2 import pass_context\nfrom starlette.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\n\ndef relative_url(from_path: str, to_path: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The relative reference that, resolved from `from_path`, yields `to_path`.\"\"\"\n    base = from_path if from_path.endswith(\"/\") else posixpath.dirname(from_path)\n    return posixpath.relpath(to_path, base or \"/\")\n\n\n@pass_context\ndef _rel(context, to_path: str) -> str:\n    return relative_url(context[\"request\"].url.path, to_path)\n\n\ndef install(templates: Jinja2Templates) -> Jinja2Templates:\n    \"\"\"Expose `rel('/admin/dashboard')` to templates rendered with a request.\"\"\"\n    templates.env.globals[\"rel\"] = _rel\n    return templates\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_page_by_slug(slug: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT * FROM pages WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)\n        ).fetchone()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef list_pages() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages ORDER BY slug\").fetchall()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef update_page(slug: str, *, title: str, body_md: str, status: str, author_id: int) -> None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"UPDATE pages\n               SET title = ?, body_md = ?, status = ?, author_id = ?,\n                   updated_at = datetime('now')\n               WHERE slug = ?\"\"\",\n            (title, body_md, status, author_id, slug),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef slug_is_valid(slug: str) -> bool:\n    return slug not in _RESERVED_SLUGS and re.fullmatch(SLUG_PATTERN, slug) is not None\n\n\n# News owns these export filenames: news.html is the index and\n# news-<slug>.html is each post. A Page slugged \"news\" or \"news-…\" would write\n# to the same file, so Pages may not use them.\nNEWS_INDEX_SLUG = \"news\"\nNEWS_PREFIX = \"news-\"\n\n\ndef is_news_slug(slug: str) -> bool:\n    return slug == NEWS_INDEX_SLUG or slug.startswith(NEWS_PREFIX)\n\n\ndef pages_conflicting_with_news() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"Existing Pages whose slug now falls in the News namespace. Reported\n    only; nothing is renamed or deleted.\"\"\"\n    return [p for p in list_pages() if is_news_slug(p[\"slug\"])]\n\n\ndef create_page(slug: str, *, title: str, body_md: str, status: str, author_id: int) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Insert a new page.    conn.close()\n\n\ndef delete_page(slug: str) -> None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,))\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef list_posts() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first; id breaks ties between posts created in the same second.\"\"\"\n    conn = {"parentUuid":"b89954da-b7fb-434e-82e8-2136d07a62d6","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"bf41cbd0-9b6f-4cb6-b4f0-9851f6e5a70f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Nk7poRJ6aGqpYhXhqBKV4J","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"T04: News posts. Editor drafts; only the Manager publishes.\n\nSeam 1 (HTTP): role checks on every route, including hand-crafted POSTs.\nSeam 2 (export): render_site() writes news.html and news-<slug>.html for\npublished posts only.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef _csrf(client, path):\n    response = client.get(path)\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    return response.cookies[\"csrf_token\"]\n\n\ndef _new_post(client, slug=\"spring-menu\", title=\"Spring menu\", body=\"Hello.\", **extra):\n    csrf = _csrf(client, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    return client.post(\n        \"/admin/posts/new\",\n        data={\"slug\": slug, \"title\": title, \"body_md\": body, \"csrf_token\": csrf, **extra},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_news_post_as_a_draft(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = _new_post(editor)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    post = db.get_post_by_slug(\"spring-menu\")\n    assert post[\"status\"] == \"draft\"\n    assert post[\"author_id\"] is not None\n\n\ndef test_editor_cannot_create_a_published_post_by_forging_status(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    _new_post(editor, status=\"published\")\n    assert db.get_post_by_slug(\"spring-menu\")[\"status\"] == \"draft\"\n\n\ndef _edit(client, slug, **data):\n    csrf = _csrf(client, f\"/admin/posts/{slug}\")\n    return client.post(\n        f\"/admin/posts/{slug}\",\n        data={\"title\": \"Spring menu\", \"body_md\": \"Updated.\", \"csrf_token\": csrf, **data},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n\n\ndef test_editor_edits_a_draft_and_it_stays_a_draft_on_every_save(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    _new_post(editor)\n    first = _edit(editor, \"spring-menu\", body_md=\"First edit.\", status=\"published\")\n    second = _edit(editor, \"spring-menu\", body_md=\"Second edit.\", status=\"published\")\ndef get_post_by_slug(slug: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM posts WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef create_post(\n    slug: str, *, title: str, body_md: str, author_id: int | None,\n    created_at: str | None = None,\n) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Insert a new News post as a draft. Returns False if the slug is taken.\n\n    `created_at` (\"YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS\") is for seed data that carries a real\n    date; the admin routes never pass it.\n    \"\"\"\n    conn = get_connection()\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"\"\"INSERT INTO posts (slug, title, body_md, author_id, created_at)\n               VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, COALESCE(?, datetime('now')))\"\"\",\n            (slug, title, body_md, author_id, created_at),\n        )\n        conn.commit()\n        return True\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        return False\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef update_post(slug: str, *, title: str, body_md: str, author_id: int) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Update a post's content. Status is deliberately not a parameter: only\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{% block css_href %}{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}{% endblock %}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header>\n    <a href=\"{% block home_href %}{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}{% endblock %}\">{{ title }}</a>\n    {% if nav_links %}\n      <nav>\n        <ul>{% for link in nav_links %}<li><a href=\"{{ link.href }}\">{{ link.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n      </nav>\n    {% endif %}\n  </header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"e9cf7ddf-0a74-4f18-ad74-27821289e684","timestamp":"2026-10-13T03:23:09.148Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"T04: News posts.
